Classification emerges from the conjunction of form, function, and development.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Consecrated Offering","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_1ea18435c673eba161bc","text":"Goods and obligations move between parties through ownership, valuation, replacement, compensation, gift, or offering.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B007","root_000532/B008","root_000532/B012","root_000745/B004","root_001064/B004","root_001119/B001","root_001525/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Sky, Rain, Wind, and Enduring Growth","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_216bb0d9fd8709f581ff","text":"overhead sky `س م و:B004/m01`; cloud and rain `س م و:B004/m02`; layered raincloud `ر ب ب:B008/m01`; persistent cloud or wind `ر ب ب:B007/m02`; gentle south wind `ن ع م:B009/m01`; enduring green plant `ر ب ب:B012/m01`; old established palm `ع و ن:B004/m01`; irrigation `غ ي ر:B001/m02`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:The Reputed Captain's Ceremonial Bearing","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_21f68031dfa8aaf635e0","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); 1:2 `رَبِّ` (`ر ب ب`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Petition for Aid and Direction","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_22f1923f8955ebb1db4e","text":"Aid supplies the enabling force, guidance supplies the directing action, the path supplies its object, and straightness supplies the desired state. gentle guidance `ه د ي:B001/m01`; the traversable path `ص ر ط:B001/m01`; uprightness and rectitude `ق و م:B008/m01`; mercy enacted as benefaction `ر ح م:B001/m02`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Worship and Obedient Submission","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_559780ef75bbdc5fbb95","text":"A dependent community turns toward a sovereign divine addressee through worship, service, obedience, and petition.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Hunt, Flight, and Scattered Routes","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_591679f7700befffaefa","text":"Departure initiates pursuit, flight breaks collective formation, and diverging roads spatialize the resulting separation. Leadership is thus rendered as a spatial relation before it becomes a social one.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Sky, Rain, Wind, and Enduring Growth","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5d2664ac314db36bb3ac","text":"Layered cloud gathers the water-bearing material, persistence and wind describe weather movement, irrigation delivers water to the ground, and green plant and old palm display growth across both season and duration.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Duration, Pace, and Praised Attainment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5d457486d3a3e75b5fa7","text":"1:2 `ٱلْحَمْدُ` and `رَبِّ` (`ح م د`, `ر ب ب`); 1:4 `يَوْمِ` (`ي و م`); 1:5 `نَعْبُدُ` (`ع ب د`); 1:6 `ٱهْدِنَا` (`ه د ي`); 1:7 `أَنْعَمْتَ` (`ن ع م`)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Petition for Aid and Direction","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_5d888b1abfdfbf060876","text":"A dependent community turns toward a sovereign divine addressee through worship, service, obedience, and petition.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Cohesion, Mainstay, and Upright Members","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_60a5e75ed45312b1fa21","text":"A structure or undertaking remains standing because material cohesion, a firm joint, and upright supporting members carry its load.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Compassionate Provision and Nurture","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_626fe85ddc2b32728359","text":"Compassion is the motive, progressive nurture and guardianship are the continuing operations, and food, equipment, and assistance are the practical means. Blessing names the resulting condition of the cared-for dependent.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Rivalry, Resistance, and Repeated War","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6aebca18ebb5e00dc8f0","text":"1:1 `بِسْمِ` (`س م و`); scattered groups and routes `ع ب د:B010/m01`; loss from a holder `ض ل ل:B003/m01`; mutual confrontation `ق و م:B014/m01`; hunters setting out in pursuit `س م و:B006/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Bodily Markers of Maturity and Reproduction","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_7052945ee4dac86ba5e3","text":"Bodily marks, sexual maturity, reproductive capacity, and age make a living body classifiable.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Anger, Mourning, Vengeance, and Redress","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_70b8604b6658fc55430c","text":"Injury activates anger, death or irreversible loss introduces grief, and retaliation offers one outcome.
